# Skills · A · part 194

793 entries, annonsering to anofox-similarity-aas. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **annonsering**: Planerar och granskar betald annonsering i exempelvis Google Ads, Meta Ads och LinkedIn Ads utifrån affärsmål, ekonomi, målgrupp, spårning och landningssida. Använd vid "annonsering", "paid media", "PPC", "Google Ads", "Meta Ads", "LinkedIn Ads", "kampanjstruktur", "budstrategi", "annonsbudget" eller "sökord och negativa sökord". Starta eller ändra aldrig en kampanj utan uttryckligt godkännande. ([Matthias957/svenska-marknadsforingsskills](https://github.com/Matthias957/svenska-marknadsforingsskills/tree/HEAD/skills/annonsering) · ★2)
- **annonsmaterial**: Tar fram och granskar annonskoncept, budskap, copy, bild- och videobriefar samt kreativa varianter för betalda kanaler. Använd vid "annonsmaterial", "ad creative", "annonscopy", "annonsvinklar", "kreativ strategi", "bildannonser", "videoannonser", "hooks" eller "creative testing". Bevara verkliga produktfakta och skapa inte påhittade påståenden, omdömen eller resultat. ([Matthias957/svenska-marknadsforingsskills](https://github.com/Matthias957/svenska-marknadsforingsskills/tree/HEAD/skills/annonsmaterial) · ★2)
- **annoq-bugfix**: Playbook for fixing a bug somewhere in the AnnoQ pipeline (data-builder → database → api-v2 → site). Use when a value is wrong/missing, indexing fails, a query breaks, or the UI misbehaves, and you need to localize the failing stage before patching. Routes work to the correct component repo. ([USCbiostats/annoq-proj](https://github.com/USCbiostats/annoq-proj/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/annoq-bugfix))
- **annoq-config**: Playbook for updating a configuration in the AnnoQ pipeline (data-builder → database → api-v2 → site). Use when changing env vars, ES mappings/index settings, Docker Compose, SLURM batch settings, endpoint URLs, or the annotation tree — where a safe, validated change matters more than code. ([USCbiostats/annoq-proj](https://github.com/USCbiostats/annoq-proj/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/annoq-config))
- **annoq-data-build**: Playbook for the annoq-data-builder post-WGSA build — after WGSA/WGSAdd has produced base annotation VCFs, add PANTHER/GO/Reactome/Enhancer functional annotations (Java module) and the HRC mapping columns (TOPMed), then generate and distribute the tree/mappings/pickle files consumed by annoq-database, annoq-api-v2 and both stage-4 site repos (annoq-site-v2, annoq-site). Use when running or documenting the data-builder stage, regenerating anno_tree.json / annoq_mappings.json / doc_type.pkl, or adding annotation columns to the VCF. ([USCbiostats/annoq-proj](https://github.com/USCbiostats/annoq-proj/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/annoq-data-build))
- **annoq-doc-sync**: Keep AnnoQ documentation in sync across repos when something changes. Many repos (annoq-proj, api-v2, annoq-py, AnnoQR, SNPWay, annoq-site, annoq-site-v2) restate the SAME api-v2 facts — endpoints, pagination/field limits, query modes, the 500+ attribute set, the annotation tree. Use whenever the API contract or another shared fact is modified, or as a periodic audit, to find every place that documents it and update them together so they don't drift. ([USCbiostats/annoq-proj](https://github.com/USCbiostats/annoq-proj/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/annoq-doc-sync))
- **annoq-feature**: Playbook for implementing a new feature in the AnnoQ pipeline (data-builder → database → api-v2 → site). Use when adding an annotation field/source, a new query/filter capability, or UI functionality that may span multiple component repos. Helps plan and sequence cross-repo changes. ([USCbiostats/annoq-proj](https://github.com/USCbiostats/annoq-proj/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/annoq-feature))
- **annota-annotate**: 论文智能标注。使用两阶段工作流：先提取全文理解内容，再对目标句子精确标注。 支持按语义分类标注（结果=绿色、方法=蓝色、不足=红色、贡献=紫色）。 无具体指令时自动执行四色全覆盖标注（每类至少3-5处）。 触发词：annotate、highlight、标注、高亮、mark this paper、标出论文中的 ([dengls24/annota](https://github.com/dengls24/annota/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/annota-annotate) · ★20)
- **annota-read**: 论文全套深度阅读。一个命令完成：交互式确认阅读深度 → 四色全覆盖标注 → 结构化笔记 → 可选审稿意见。 这是 Annota 的主入口，适合"帮我读这篇论文"这类开放式请求。 触发词：read paper、帮我读、阅读论文、深度阅读、read this、帮我分析这篇 ([dengls24/annota](https://github.com/dengls24/annota/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/annota-read) · ★20)
- **annota-review**: 以学术审稿人视角审阅论文，输出结构化评审意见。 可指定会议级别（MICRO/ISCA/HPCA/ASPLOS/DAC/ISSCC 等）， 输出包括分维度评分、优缺点分析、逐条修改建议。 触发词：review paper、审阅论文、paper review、审稿、评审 ([dengls24/annota](https://github.com/dengls24/annota/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/annota-review) · ★20)
- **annota-summarize**: 为论文生成结构化阅读笔记，自动保存为子笔记。 提取核心贡献、方法、实验结果、不足和启发，生成中英文双语笔记。 触发词：summarize paper、论文笔记、读书笔记、paper note、总结这篇论文 ([dengls24/annota](https://github.com/dengls24/annota/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/annota-summarize) · ★20)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/annotation-candidate-comparative-scoring) · 3 copies)

- **annotation-candidate-true-positive-classification**: Use when evaluating whether predicted-vs-measured CCS differences can separate true from false annotation candidates, e.g. sweeping ΔCCS thresholds across 10, 7, 5, 3, 1, or 0 ppm mass windows to compute ROC AUC for CCSP-based filtering.
